	Desc = "Rebirths Aprox: 0 / Hora | 0 / Día | 0 / Semana | 0 / Mes"
})

local Paragraph10 = Section7:Paragraph({ Title = "Promedio", Desc = "Promedio De Rebirths: 0 / Hora | 0 / Día | 0 / Semana | 0 / Mes" })

State.RebirthTrack = {
	averageStartRebirths = Refs.rebirthsStat.Value,
	averageStartedAt = 0,
	initialRebirths = Refs.rebirthsStat.Value,
	lastUpdateRebirths = Refs.rebirthsStat.Value,
	lastUpdateTime = 0,
	pausedTime = 0,
	startTime = 0,
	tracking = false
}

task.spawn(function(...)
	task.wait(0.1)
	Paragraph5:SetDesc("Tiempo: 0d 0h 0m 0s")
	task.wait(0.1)

end)

task.spawn(function(...)
	task.wait(10)
	task.wait(10)

end)

Section7:Toggle({
	Title = "Fast Rebirths",
	Value = false,
	Callback = function(state, arg573)
		if state then
			getgenv().AutoFarming = state
			Paragraph5:SetDesc("Tiempo: 0d 0h 0m 0s")
			tostring(math.floor(tonumber(Refs.rebirthsStat.Value))):sub(1, 1)
			local result2 = tostring(math.floor(tonumber(Refs.rebirthsStat.Value))):reverse()
			local result3 = result2:gsub("(%d%d%d)", "%1,")
			local result4 = result3:reverse()
			local result5 = result4:gsub("^,", "")
			Paragraph6:SetDesc("Rebirths: " .. "" .. result5)
			Paragraph7:SetDesc("Rebirths Ganados: 0")
			Paragraph8:SetDesc("Estatus: Activo")
			Paragraph9:SetDesc("Rebirths Aprox: 0 / Hora | 0 / Día | 0 / Semana | 0 / Mes")
			Paragraph10:SetDesc("Promedio De Rebirths: 0 / Hora | 0 / Día | 0 / Semana | 0 / Mes")
			task.spawn(function(...)
				local ultimatesFolder = Players.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("ultimatesFolder")
				ultimatesFolder:FindFirstChild("Golden Rebirth")
			end)
		else
			getgenv().AutoFarming = false
			Paragraph8:SetDesc("Estatus: Inactivo")
		end
	end
})

			Humanoid7.JumpPower = 0
			Humanoid7.AutoRotate = false
			Humanoid7:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics)
			local connection6 = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime3)
				Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			end)
			local connection7 = Players.LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character4)
			end)
		else
			connection6:Disconnect()
			connection7:Disconnect()

			Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			Humanoid8.WalkSpeed = 250
			Humanoid8.JumpPower = 50
			Humanoid8.AutoRotate = true

Tab7:Toggle({
	Title = "Ver Vida De Todos",
	Callback = function(arg674, arg675)
		local players4 = Players:GetPlayers()
		for i105, v113 in ipairs(players4) do
			local Humanoid12 = v113.Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
			local Head = v113.Character:WaitForChild("Head", 5)
			local HP_BILLBOARD = v113.Character:FindFirstChild("HP_BILLBOARD")
			HP_BILLBOARD:Destroy()
			local BillboardGui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
			BillboardGui.Name = "HP_BILLBOARD"
			BillboardGui.Size = UDim2.new(6, 0, 1.2, 0)
			BillboardGui.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.5, 0)
			BillboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true
			BillboardGui.MaxDistance = 200
			BillboardGui.Parent = Head
			local Frame = Instance.new("Frame")
			Frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new()
			Frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
			Frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
			Frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
			Frame.Parent = BillboardGui
			local Frame2 = Instance.new("Frame")
			Frame2.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50)
			Frame2.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
			Frame2.BorderSizePixel = 0
			Frame2.Parent = Frame
			local TextLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			TextLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			TextLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
			TextLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
			TextLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
			TextLabel.TextSize = 18
			TextLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
			TextLabel.Parent = Frame
			Humanoid12.HealthChanged:Connect(function(health)
				Frame4.Size = UDim2.new(math.clamp((Humanoid12.Health / math.max(Humanoid12.MaxHealth, 1)), 0, 1), 0, 1, 0)
			end)
			Humanoid12.Died:Once(function()
			end)
			Frame2.Size = UDim2.new(math.clamp((Humanoid12.Health / math.max(Humanoid12.MaxHealth, 1)), 0, 1), 0, 1, 0)
		end
	end
})
